Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 05:13:33 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How voting at private website is useless for activist/voters and huge money maker for website owner?
.
Central Govt has made a website named as newindia dot in, on which one can put causes and ask all voters across India to vote. The voters may put their twitterid as username and may put voterid in their twitter profile, and thus viewers can see which voter-ids voted for which causes. But website doesnt reveal voter's identity, except for namde put by the voter himself
.
Now newindia dot in admin can anyday cancel a cause. So efforts of activists will get wasted,. But some advantage will come ---- such deletion of causes will expose PM, because finally, it was his website and whole staff comes under his command
.
But if some private website owners sells the website or takes cash and deletes votes or deletes proposals, then PM or elitemen dont lose any reputation. The website owner can tell all that his copy is correct, and copy of voting long of everyone else is fake and tempered. The voters will blame A or B, but reputation of PM and elitemen remains intact.
.
So in private website too, there can be loss of voting record and loss of uploaded proposals, and same can be on newindia dot in. But on newindia dot in, if that happens, then PM's reputation goes down for his own staff's nefarious actions. And that is a huge plus point of TRUE activists,. Whereas when private website owner sells out, activists wasted their time and PM's reputation of reputation of PM's sponors remains intact
.
Also, many private website talk of transparency etc. And that website does NOT give out name of its owner or voter-id of the owner !! So much for their belief in transparency !!!.
.
So imo, activists should NOT waste time in voting on a private website. By doing so, they are ONLY making the website owner rich.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 07:46:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(1) what do we activists "gain" if a proposal on newindia dot in is voted by say 1 crore voters and then it is deleted by Pmo staff? - we gain a LOT
.
(2) what do we activists loose if 1 crore voters vote on a private website and and owner changes voting record or deletes the proposal? --- we activists loose a lot. Even if many have voting record's copy
.
---
.
(1) Say we upload a proposal on newindia and say we convince 1000 or 1 crore proposal supporters to vote on newindia. Say Pmo staff deletes that proposal. In that case, reputation of Pmo staff and PM himself in the eyes of ALL voters who had seen that proposal goes down for their own crime. And thats a huge gain for activists --- they were able to CONVINCE voters that PM is defunct
.
(2) Say an activist uploads a proposal on a PRIVATE website and activists manage to convince 1000 or 1 crore supporters to vote for that proposal on that private website. Say that private website owner gives copy of who voted for whom to all. Then one day, the private website owner deletes that proposal. Then what does PM loose ? NOTHING. His reputation doesnt go down at all. Who do elitemen lose? Only some money that they paid to website owner. But their reputation doesnt decrease as none can prove that money was given. And private website owner can keep silence on other activists' claims that private website owner deleted the copy. Voter can believe silence of website owner or believe the activist who is complaining about deletion. The activists lose due to their infighting. neither PM nor elitemen lose anything.
.
----
.
So voting on private website is waste of activists' and voters time. Whereas voting on govt website will add strength to draft's movement OR lessen reputation of PM and PM's sponsors. So I request activists NOT to waste time in publicizing private voting wensites.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 08:03:04 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Many activists, who call themselves "recallists" are claiming that #JurySys3 draft uploaded by myself at https://newindia.in/causes/JurySys3 dot in will WORSEN India !!!
.
I dismiss them as LIARS
.
Here are some questions you may ask them.
.
(1) Say JurySys3 is extended to all violent crimes, all IPC crimes, all civil disputes such as landlord tenant disputesm labor disputes, family disputes etc
.
(2) Currently there are some 3 crore pending cases which are to be resolved by judges
.
(3) Say JurySys3 comes.
.
In how many of these 3 crores cases, as per their OPINION, will JurySys3 deliver worse judgment than what judges will be giving (or delaying for years)
.
----------------
.
My observation was ----
.
(1) They evade STATISTICAL analysis of how JurySys3 will impact lakhs and crores of cases
.
(2) Next, they will pull out some niche case, put a claim that elitemen will pay paidmediamen to run a massive campaign to confuse Jurors and will manage to get a bad judgement !! Well, with such massive spending, elitemen who pay paidmedia can also bribe the judges and get a bad judgement. So in such niche case, it is equal-equal in worst case. Even in niche case it doesnt work eg recently a US Jury fined $280 million to Monsanto for hiding the fact that chemicals used in their herbicide can cause cancer,. Why could media spending confuse these Jurors?
.
All in all, media campaign has to be nationwide or statewide. And to create a frenzy, media has to spend 100s of crores fo rupees and cover same news again and again for several days. There are 3 crore cases. Elitemen cant create media frenzy for even 0.01% cases. And even in that case, more Jurors will NOT bother to see what paidmedia says, when parties in case are presenting their own arguments
.
Paidmedia works when paidmedia has monopoly over audience. In JurySys3, the parties which are presenting case are ALSO giving infoation and so before Jurors, paidmedia does NOT have monopoly.
.
So arguments that #JurySys3-law-draft is worse than existing judge system, that too made by people who claim they are recallists (!) , is LIE and I dismiss these people as LIARS.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 14:51:53 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How anti-recallists or psuedo-recallists use exaggerated strength of paidmediamen create fear against proposed RTR-law-drafts - by creating a FALSE scenario that elitemen can create thousands and lakhs of paidmedia campaigns !!!
.
One of the arguments of anti-recallists (or psuedo-recallists) use against all proposed RTR-law-drafts is --- (a) RTR-law-drafts and JurySys-law-drafts put power in hands of voters, and (b) paidmediamen can manipulate voter opinions and Juror opinions and so RTR/Jury-law-drafts MUST be postponed till XYZ conditions are met !!
.
Their main plank is --- they show paidmediamen as supremely powerful, more powerful than 10 Gods, who can convince voters that day is night and night is day !!!
.
And THEY IGNORE THE COST THAT PAIDMEDIA CAMPAIGNS NEED.
.
When RTR-law-drafts comes, voters get power to expel / replace / retain / bringback 600 District Education Officers, 600 District Police Commissioners, 600 District judges, 600 District Public Prosecutors i.e. some 25 nodal officers in each district i.e. about 25 * 600 = 150,000 District level offices. And Juries get power to expel all 10000s of Municipal / State officers below the rank of District Nodal Officers such as collectors.
.
And voters get power to replace 30 CMs, 30 High Court Chief judges, 30 State Chief Public Prosecutors, all High Court judges and so on. So there are 30 states, and some 50 to 200 officers / judges in each state (depending on size of State's apex bureaucracy / judiciary ). So at State level , voters get procedure to expel / replace / retain / bringback 30 * 100 = 3000 state level apex officials
.
And at national level they almost all Ministers, apex PSU chairman, SCCj, SCjs, RBI Chairman, RBI Directors, SBI Chairman etc become recallable/replaceable. So at national level, some 20 to 50 positions become recallable / replaceable.
.
Now can paidmediamen manipulate opinion on ALL positions?
.
And if they fail to manipulate opinions even on 10% positions, then the 100% good individuals at apex, using their official powers, can dig out all data and expose remaining 90%. One good judge is enough to expose all nepotism in all courts,. One good Minister is enough to expose murky deals of ALL Ministers.
.
One clean District Education cant get data of other DEOs, but he can surely frame RIGHT QUESTIONS and send to all voters across states, that will expose all DEOs. Worse, a good DEO can also frame right questions that expose ALL police chiefs, all judges, all public prosecutors etc and send these questions to all voters across District / State.
.
So even if paidmedia is successful in putting elitemen's puppets in 90% places, the remaining 10% will do so much expose in 2-3 months, that subsequent recalls will ensure replacement of all their puppets.
.
So once RTR/Jury-law-drafts are printed, elitemen will need to run LAKHS of paidmedia campaign to stop good guys from coming from 100% of 1000s of district level / state level positions.
.
Each paidmedia campaign has to run for several days. And for all those days, 24 hour coverage has to be given. eg consider paidmediamen campaign that was run for Anna during 4-apr-2011 and 11-apr-2011. The campaign had to be run almost 24 hours for seven days. And then weeks and months or coverage had to be given to install ArKe as a leader. The costs of each campaign is 1000s of crores of rupees
.
So when RTR comes over 1000s of district level positions across India, and 10s of State level positions across India, and 10s of National Level positions, the elitemen will need to run 1000s of campaigns every MONTH to ensure that ONLY their puppets come on ALL positions.
.
======================
.
Cost estimates of 1000s of paidmedia campaigns
------------------------------------------------------
.
So lets do how much will it cost elitemen to stop good guys from coning at all positions across India
.
district level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 50 crore at least. state level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 1000 cr average
national level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 20000 crores. .
.
district level positions = at least 30
state level positions = at least 30
national level positions = at least 20
.
number of districts = 600
number of states = 20 (club many small states as one)
nation = 1
.
so cost of all district level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 30 positions per district * 600 district * Rs 50 crore per campaign
= 18000 * Rs 50 crores
= Rs 900,000 crores
.
so cost of all state level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 30 positions per state * 20 states * Rs 1000 crore per campaign
= 600 * Rs 1000 crores
= Rs 600,000 crores
.
cost of all national level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 20 positions across India * 1 nation * Rs 20000 crore per campaign
= Rs 400,000 crores
.
Total = Rs 900,000 + Rs 600,000 + Rs 400,000 = Rs 29,00,000 crores !!
.
This is close to ALL taxes collected by center / state / municipalities across India. And this is almost 15% of India's total GDP !!
.
Well, elitemen cant spend even 1/10th of the money they need to block all good guys and putting all their puppets at even 10% of the district / state / national positions.
.
And say elitemen and paidmediamen managed to put their puppets on these positions. But in RTR-drafts, the voters can replace / expel anyday !!! So elitemen will need to spend crores of rupees PER POSITION every week to hide facts about good guys and promote lies about their bad guys. Now you multiply amounts, by number of positions per state / district and number of states / district . No one has such money
.
Another way to analyse is to take per citizen PER POSITION cost. Each citizen has now power to replace / expel / retain bringback people at 80 positions across distroict / state / India. If paidmedia expense per position per voter is Rs 100, then cost to manipulate opnions of 50 crore voters for 80 positions is
= 80 positions * 50 crore voters * Rs 100 per voter per position
= Rs 400,000 crores AT LEAST.
.
And recall / replacement is possible anyday. So everyweek, paidmediamen will need to print lies about puppets and lies about good guys. So the cost now becomes alnost Rs 400,000 PER WEEK !!! . No one has that kind of money/
.
===================
.
So how do elitemen prevail using paidmediamen today?
----------------------------------------------------------------------
.
A voters wants a good PM / CM. And so he has no choice but to vote for MP / MLA candidate of the party to which hos preferred PM candidate belongs to.
.
So defacto, whole nation is electing only one PM, and only 25-30 CMs only !! The election of 543 MPs and 5000 MLAs is only a formality to elect that one MP and 25 - 30 CMs !!! And election of PM / CM comes only once in 5 years,
.
So number of POSITIONS for which voters EFFECTIVELY vote today are few --- only 2 per state (PM / CM). So today, elitemen and paidmediamen prevail due to lack of voting opportunities that voters have.
.
And at PM / CM level, voters dont have "word of mouth" to know performance,. eg Voters of Ahmedabad can know about performance of DEO of Rajkot thru his friends etc in Rajkot. So PAIDMEDIA campaign will have zero effect in DEO campaigns - because voters have firsthand info from people they know. But in PM / CM, population is so large, and number of tasks are so many --- that one cant evaluate. So one goes by 2-3 major problems like crimes, law-order, islamist problem etc, and casts his votes. And so paidmediamen prevail.
.
=============
.
IOW, all the claims that paidmediamen can manipulate opinions of 90 voters for even 10% of the positions of DEO, district police chiefs, district judges, district public prosecutors, state HCCj, HCjs, CMs, PM, RBI Governor, SBI Chairman, ONGC Chairman is just a WHITE LIE. Elietemen dont have money to manipulate so many opnions,.
.
And claim that elitemen can manipulate opinions of crores Jurors in 3 crore Jury Trial , thru paidmedia, is a bigger LIE. And LIE becomes clear when costs are calculated per trial. I will explain in some other posts.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 15:25:42 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Electricity consumption kwhr growth in apr-jun-2018 = 2.64%
.
Increase in consumption in kilograms of petroleuum products = 4%
.
And economic growth rate = 8.2% !!!
.
The mystery is
.
(1a) all sales are first calculated at CURRENT prices. Growth = 13%
.
(1b) Then inflation is calulated = 5%
.
(1c) Then real growth rate is calculated as growth rate on current prices minus inflation = 8%
.
(1d) IOW, by under calculating inflation, real growth rate is reported as higher number
.
(2) Electronics/computers/hardware growth rate increased because a lot of chinese / korean companies have moved into India. And all their low level ASSEMBLY is getting counted as manufacturing !!!
.
For data , see links in first comment

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 01 2018 17:22:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

how/why GST causes most damage to "one man show" (or few men show) type manufacturing units, and wealthtax causes least damage to them?
.
Many highly productive units in manufacturing are one man show or a few men show, where 1 person or 2-3 close friends / relatives manage all workers, they do all accounting, do all reporting to govt etc and also do do all sales / marketing etc
.
Now, such "one man show" or "few men show" may survive in SERVICES, because they dont have a supply chain and inventory to manage.And so inventory investment and investment of unsold goods, and also manage a time bound production schedule are non-issue or lesser issues in services or softwares type "goods". They are also lesser issues in trading.
.
Now in GST, eWayBill and now EPF and many other laws, compliance restrictions are so heavy that such "one man show" or "few men show" will collapse. Because there is no way they can meet compliance burden that GST and eWayBill system imposes on them.
.
And so what does nation lose? If these small "one man show" or "few men show" are shut down? Their business will pass to large companies. So what does nation lose?
.
Well, first loss to nation is efficiency. These one man show or few men show MANUFACTURING units are highly productive . If tax laws kill them, it then it would be a loss to whole economy
,.
Next, these one men show or few men show are also big trainning grounds for many many semi-skilled workers,. These owners supervise workers on hour by hour basis, and try to get best work with least damage,. The workers do feel suffocation under them, but workers learn the trader and gain skills under these owners. IOW, these small units are also big SKILL ADDERS to nation's population.
.
GST and eWayBill System is slaughtering all such "one man shows" and "few men shows" in MANUFACTURING. And as a result, our efficiency in as a economy will go down
.
We are already facing music in textiles and garments. In our DOMESTIC market, share of Indian manufacturers is DECREASING and share of Bangladeshi (i.e. chinese) is increasing !!! And we will face more music in almost all manufacturing !!
.
SOLUTION?
.
Solution I propose is to CancelGST and levy WealthTax.
.
If you support wealth tax, then pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/WealthTax . And pls click my coverpic, see description, and search for WealthTax for more.

  • https://newindia.in/cau

GST --- truck company clerk forgets to put truck number. penalty= Rs 1.32 crore only !! And HCj agrees with penalty !!! e-way बिल पर ट्रक का नंबर लिखना भूल जाने पर पेनेल्टी चुकाइए। पेनल्टी की राशि है -- सिर्फ 1.32 करोड़ !! (see Hindi translation later)
.
Transporter forgets to add truck's number in SoMoke's GST's eWayBill. penalty imposed was Rs 1.32 crore only !! cost of shipment was Rs 1.20 crores. Truck cost = may be Rs 30 lakhs . And GST tribunal and High Court judge CONFIRM the fine !!! (19-july-2018 news). Now transporter will have to go to supreme judges.
.
----------
.
see more at --- " Error in e-way bill filing proves costly, leads to Rs 1.32 crore penalty for Transport Company "
.
https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/small-biz/policy-trends/error-in-e-way-bill-filing-proves-costly-leads-to-rs-1-32-crore-penalty-for-transport-company/articleshow/65049910.cms
.
---------------
.
SoMoKe and congress/rss/aap workers have designed eWayBill system to crush small manufacturers etc. And sadly, it is also working !!
.
Now large companies have end to end computerization, and so such errors on their part will be neglible. But many "one man show" or "few men show" type companies make many such book keeping errors, and will get slaughtered raw !!! So they will be exiting, and all their business will pass into hands of large companies, and mostly MNCs or USUK-puppets like Tata, Godrej, Birla etc.
.
Also, tribunal and high court judges have been ordered to confirm all these penalties. So only option left is to appeal in supreme. Now a large company will hire some SCj's son lawyer as retainer, pay him Rs 1 crore for ALL 100 - 200 cases a year, and get all fines lowered. But a small company will have only 1-2 cases a year and so he will need to pay full fee -- Rs 5 lakhs per case in general. So consider 10000 small companies. They will have to pay fee of TOTAL Rs 100s of crores a year. while consider 10 large companies. They will be paying a fee of only Rs 10 crore total. So all in all, small companies will end up paying HUGE fines or HUGE lawyer fees for their clerical mistakes. While large companies will end up paying only a small amount as lawyer fee. Suffices to say --- small company owners will all get slaughetered and mashed.
.
This was the intenion of SoMoke and congress/rss/aap workers in bringing GST and then brining eWayBill System.
.
SOLUTION?
.
Solution I propose is --- CancelGST and instead levy WealthTax.
.
If you support wealth tax, then pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/WealthTax . And pls click my coverpic, see description, and search for WealthTax for more.
.
GST --- e-way बिल पर ट्रक का नंबर लिखना भूल जाने पर पेनेल्टी चुकाइए। पेनल्टी की राशि है -- सिर्फ 1.32 करोड़ !!
.
मध्य प्रदेश का ट्रांसपोर्टर eWay बिल में ट्रक नंबर डालना भूल गया था। उस पर विभाग ने 1.32 करोड़ की पेनल्टी लगा दी। शिपमेंट की लागत 1.20 करोड़ है !! ट्रक की कीमत 15 लाख है !! और हाई कोर्ट ने मामला सुनने के बाद पेनल्टी चुकाने के आदेश दिए है !!! अब वादी सुप्रीम कोर्ट में धक्के खा रहे है।

न्यूज लिंक -- https://goo.gl/rna4Jy

सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल ने GST एवं e-way बिल को इस तरह से डिजाइन किया है जिससे छोटे कारोबारियों को बाजार से बाहर किया जा सके। और उनका यह तरीका काम भी कर रहा है !!
.
बड़ी कम्पनियों का सारा कारोबार कम्प्यूटराइज्ड है , अत: उनमे इस तरह की गड़बड़ी होने की सम्भावना लगभग नगण्य है। लेकिन अकेले आदमी की कम्पनी या सिर्फ कुछ व्यक्तियों द्वारा संचालित की जाने वाली कम्पनी में एकाउंटिंग की गड़बड़ी होने से वे अपना बिजनेस खड़े खड़े गवां देंगे !! इनके बाजार से बाहर हो जाने पर छोटी कम्पनियों का कारोबार बड़ी कम्पनियों के कब्जे में चला जाएगा। और इन बड़ी कम्पनियों में ज्यादातर कम्पनियां या तो अमेरिकी-ब्रिटिश धनिकों की है या फिर उनकी कठपुतलियां है।
.
समाधान ?
.

.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Sep 01 2018 18:20:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

GST --- e-way बिल पर ट्रक का नंबर लिखना भूल जाने पर पेनेल्टी चुकाइए। पेनल्टी की राशि है -- सिर्फ 1.32 करोड़ !!
.
मध्य प्रदेश का ट्रांसपोर्टर eWay बिल में ट्रक नंबर डालना भूल गया था। उस पर विभाग ने 1.32 करोड़ की पेनल्टी लगा दी। शिपमेंट की लागत 1.20 करोड़ है !! ट्रक की कीमत 15 लाख है !! और हाई कोर्ट ने मामला सुनने के बाद पेनल्टी चुकाने के आदेश दिए है !!! अब वादी सुप्रीम कोर्ट में धक्के खा रहे है।
.
न्यूज लिंक -- <https://goo.gl/rna4Jy>;
.
सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल ने GST एवं e-way बिल को इस तरह से डिजाइन किया है जिससे छोटे कारोबारियों को बाजार से बाहर किया जा सके। और उनका यह तरीका काम भी कर रहा है !!
.
बड़ी कम्पनियों का सारा कारोबार कम्प्यूटराइज्ड है , अत: उनमे इस तरह की गड़बड़ी होने की सम्भावना लगभग नगण्य है। लेकिन अकेले आदमी की कम्पनी या सिर्फ कुछ व्यक्तियों द्वारा संचालित की जाने वाली कम्पनी में एकाउंटिंग की गड़बड़ी होने से वे अपना बिजनेस खड़े खड़े गवां देंगे !! इनके बाजार से बाहर हो जाने पर छोटी कम्पनियों का कारोबार बड़ी कम्पनियों के कब्जे में चला जाएगा। और इन बड़ी कम्पनियों में ज्यादातर कम्पनियां या तो अमेरिकी-ब्रिटिश धनिकों की है या फिर उनकी कठपुतलियां है।
.
समाधान ?
.
हमारे द्वारा प्रस्तावित समाधान -- GST को रद्द करके वेल्थ टेक्स लागू किया जाए।
.
यदि आप वेल्थ टेक्स का समर्थन करते है तो न्यू इण्डिया के इस लिंक पर जाकर इस क़ानून के पक्ष में वोट करें -- <https://newindia.in/causes/WealthTax>;
.

GST --- truck company clerk forgets to put truck number. penalty= Rs 1.32 crore only !! And HCj agrees with penalty !!!
.
Transporter forgets to add truck's number in SoMoke's GST's eWayBill. penalty imposed was Rs 1.32 crore only !! cost of shipment was Rs 1.20 crores. Truck cost = may be Rs 30 lakhs . And GST tribunal and High Court judge CONFIRM the fine !!! (19-july-2018 news). Now transporter will have to go to supreme judges.
.

.

.

.

.
SoMoKe and congress/rss/aap workers have designed eWayBill system to crush small manufacturers etc. And sadly, it is also working !!
.
Now large companies have end to end computerization, and so such errors on their part will be neglible. But many "one man show" or "few men show" type companies make many such book keeping errors, and will get slaughtered raw !!! So they will be exiting, and all their business will pass into hands of large companies, and mostly MNCs or USUK-puppets like Tata, Godrej, Birla etc.
.
Also, tribunal and high court judges have been ordered to confirm all these penalties. So only option left is to appeal in supreme. Now a large company will hire some SCj's son lawyer as retainer, pay him Rs 1 crore for ALL 100 - 200 cases a year, and get all fines lowered. But a small company will have only 1-2 cases a year and so he will need to pay full fee -- Rs 5 lakhs per case in general. So consider 10000 small companies. They will have to pay fee of TOTAL Rs 100s of crores a year. while consider 10 large companies. They will be paying a fee of only Rs 10 crore total. So all in all, small companies will end up paying HUGE fines or HUGE lawyer fees for their clerical mistakes. While large companies will end up paying only a small amount as lawyer fee. Suffices to say --- small company owners will all get slaughetered and mashed.
.
This was the intenion of SoMoke and congress/rss/aap workers in bringing GST and then brining eWayBill System.
.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Sep 02 2018 06:08:39 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(a) What comes first --- RTR over an officer or good behavior from him? (b) Is there is any to add clauses to make officers properly in RTR-law-drafts?
.
---
.
(a) My answer is RTR-clauses have to come first, and only then one can expect good behavior from him in general. A few officers, say 2% to 5% , may behave properly even in absence of RTR clauses. But they wont be able to do much --- because apex IAS , Ministers and judges, and also fellow IAS officers will block them from going far. And once RTR-law-draft comes, the officers who dont behave properly will be replaced by less bad ones, and so ALL will behave properly without asking. So IMO, clauses to enforce proper behavior , be disclosures or promptly attending complains, are pointless in absence of RTR-clauses,
.
(b) And once RTR-clauses come, imo, there is NO need to print clauses for good behavior, that can be implemented with by officer or his staff within a normal budget. Because officers who dont behave properly will get replaced by citizens using RTR-law-drafts.
.
As an example, consider Saatya System I have proposed for maths science education. This system is NOT part of proposed RTR-DEO-law-draft. Why not? Because imo, it is useless to add all these clauses. In absence of RTR-law-drafts, DEO will implement these clauses in worst possible way. And if RTR-law-draft is there, then DEO who doesnt implement Saatya System within available budget will get replaced by the one who does. So whole task of adding 100s of clauses to implement Saatya System is imo useless. So Saatya System details are NOT mentioned in #RtrDeo3 law-draft I have proposed.
.
-----------
.
So one can propose to add 10s and 10s of clauses in drafts wrt prompt disclosures, prompt deliveries, promote replies to complaints and so on. All these clauses are useless in absence of RTR / Jury. And once RTR / Jury / Tcp come, the Grand Jurors or Jurors or voters can issue these clauses as general instructions to officers. And in most cases, officers will implement them on their own without even asking.
.
So I dismiss whole exercise of adding disclosure clause , promote complain reddressal clauses etc as useless. Those who want to add these clauses can continue their campaign.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Sep 02 2018 13:43:26 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

बहुत से कार्यकर्ता जो स्वयं को “रिकालिस्ट” कहते हैं, ये दावा करते हैं newindia.in पर जूरी सिस्टम का जो ड्राफ्ट (https://newindia.in/causes/JurySys3 )अपलोड किया है, वो लागू होते ही भारत की स्थिति और बदतर कर देगा !!!
.
मैं उन्हें झूठा कहता हूँ
.
यहाँ कुछ प्रश्न है जो आप उनसे पूछ सकते हैं—
.
1. मान लीजिये जूरी सिस्टम (#JurySys3) का विस्तार सभी हिंसक अपराधों, सभी भारतीय दंड संहिता के अंतर्गत आने वाले अपराधों, सभी नागरिक विवादों जैसे मकानमालिक—किरायेदार का विवाद, फैक्ट्री मालिक-लेबर विवाद, घरेलू विवाद आदि तक होता है
.
2. वर्तमान में अदालतों में करीब 3 करोड़ से ज्यादा मामले लंबित है जिन्हें भ्रष्ट जजों द्वारा निपटाया जायेगा
.
3. अब मनन लीजिये जूरी सिस्टम आता है
.
इन 3 करोड़ मामलों में से ऐसे कितने मामले, इन झूठे कार्यकर्ताओं के अनुसार, जूरी सिस्टम ख़राब जजमेंट देगा उन जजों के मुकाबले जो अभी जजमेंट दे रहे हैं (या वर्षों तक मामलों को लटकाए हुए हैं)?
.
-------
.
मेरी दृष्टि ये कहती हैं---
.
1. कैसे जूरी सिस्टम इन लाखों–करोड़ो मामलों पर प्रभाव डालेगा, ये कार्यकर्ता इसके संख्याकिक विश्लेषण को छुपा लेते हैं
.
2. आगे, ये कुछ आला मामलें लेकर आयेंगे, ये दावा करते हैं कि धनिक और प्रभावशाली व्यक्ति मीडियामालिकों को पैसा देकर एक देशव्यापी आन्दोलन खड़ा कर सकते हैं जिससे जूरी सदस्यों को भ्रमित किया जा सके और इससे वे एक बेकार निर्णय लेने में सफल हो जाएंगे!! वैसे, इतना खर्चा धनिक व्यक्ति पेड मीडिया की जगह जजों को घूस देकर भी एक बेकार निर्णय प्राप्त कर सकता हैं | तो ऐसे आला मामलों में, दोनों मामलों में ख़राब से ख़राब स्थिति में एक जैसा हाल है !! जबकि इन आला मामलों में ये काम नहीं करेगा उदहारण के लिए – कुछ ही समय पहले अमेरिका में जूरी ने मोंसन्तो नामक एक कीटनाशक कंपनी को ये तथ्य छुपाने के लिए कि उनकी कीटनाशक दवाओं में इस्तेमाल होने वाले रसायनों से कैंसर हो सकता है, 280 मिलियन डॉलर भरने का जुरमाना लगाया था | क्यों मीडिया इन जूरी सदस्यों को भर्मित करने के लिए खर्चा करती ?
.
कुल मिलाकर, मीडिया का प्रचार राष्ट्रीय स्तर पर होना चाहिए या राजकीय स्तर पर | और उन्माद पैदा करने के लिए, मीडिया को हजार करोड़ रूपये खर्च करने होंगे और एक ही खबर को कई दिनों तक बार बार कवर करना पड़ेगा | हमारे यहाँ 3 करोड़ केस पड़े हैं | धनिक मीडिया उन्माद मात्र 0.01% मामलों के लिए भी खड़ा नहीं कर सकते हैं और उस केस में भी, जूरी सदस्य ये ध्यान नहीं देंगे कि पेड मीडिया क्या कह रही है, जब केस में दोनों पक्ष अपने अपने तर्क प्रस्तुत कर रहे होंगे !!
.
पेड मीडिया तभी काम करती है जब पेड मीडिया की लोगों को ऊपर एकाधिकार होता है | जूरी सिस्टम में, जो पक्ष अपना केस प्रस्तुत भी जूरी सदस्यों को जानकारी दे रहे हैं, और पेड मीडिया के पास एकाधिकार नहीं है |
.
इसलिए ये तर्क कि जूरी सिस्टम कानून ड्राफ्ट, वर्तमान में चल रहे जज सिस्टम से ज्यादा ख़राब है और वो भी उन लोगो के द्वारा जो स्वयं को रिकालिस्ट बता रहे हैं, सरासर झूठ है और मैं उन्हें झूठा मानता हूँ !!

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 03 2018 13:27:09 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Can elitemen using paidmediamen damage even 1% of the 3 crore Jury Trials across India? imo, NO ; with ALL their money they have, they cant influence even 1 out 10000 small cases, or medium cases or large cases !!! How / Why?
.
In existing judge system, elitemen manage to influence almost 99% of cases thru bribes and nexuses, and hierarchy. So all those who are claiming that #JurySys3 will do damage to India --- I dismiss them as LIARS.
.
==============
.
(A) Summary :
------------
.
One of many arguments anti-Jury pro-judge people across India give against my proposed #JurySys3-law-draft, given at newindia dot in /causes/JurySys3, is that elitemen will manage to get "bad" judgments from Jurors using paidmedia campaigns ; and so #JurySys3 in India should be brought ONLY after their proposed pre-conditions and co-conditions are first met !!! Or rather , JurySys3-law-draft should not be brought at all.
.
Anti-JurySys3 people will not give their estimate of what % of the 3 crore cases can be effected by paidmedia campaigns , and how much would that cost, and whether elitemen have that kind to money to begin with. I request activists to look into NUMERICAL SCENARIOS and PAIDMEDIA COSTS, That will prove that claims that #JurySys3 will do worse than existing judge system are all false.
.
Here, I will show that their claims that elitemen can influence Jurors via paidmedia campaigns is a LIE. My method is simple --- show costs of district / state / national level campaigns, and show that they can NOT fund such campaigns for even 0.01% of the 3 crore cases.
.
==============
.
(B) why paidmedia campaigns almost ALWAYS fail in Jury Trials
----------------------------------------
.
Lets take some REAL examples.
.
(B.1) A recent example is Jury Trial where Jurors imposed fine of about $270 million on Monsanto company for not putting warning labels that their herbicide spray liquid has cancer causing chemicals. Monsanto regularly pays paidmediamen to write that their chemicals are harmless. But many doctors were able to present cases of humans as well as rabbits / mouses, which convinced Jurors that chemicals do cause cancer. Monsanto could NOT prevent doctors.etc from talking to Jurors. So mediamen's reports did indeed effect Jurors. But effect got washed away, when doctors .presented their opinions.
.
(B.2) Another example was McDonald hot coffee case of 1994. The Jurors imposed fine of about $3 million because McDonald kept selling coffee at 88 degrees centigrade, while McDonald's own Dermatologists had warned the company that the chances of third degree burns increases after 75 degree centigrade (and chances of third degree burns are neglible below 75 degrees tempreture) if there is a spill by accident. But McDonalds kept selling super hot coffee because it was increasing profits. After fine, McDonald immidiately ordered all its outlets to improve warning labels, and also reduce tempretures, thereby stopping further injuries. And here too, McDonald tried its best to use paidmediamen to convince Jurors that super hot coffee is harmless for skin. But McDonalds could NOT stop doctors from presenting their arguments in court. And simple arguments and reports of dermatologists were sufficient to inform Jurors about obviuous damages that super heat coffee causes. All in all, the paidmedia campaign failed..
.
(B.3) So why did paidmedia campaigns failed?
.
(B.4) Because in Jury Trial, both parties get opportunity to present their statements. And paidmediamen can also present their paid stories. But paidmediamen can NOT stop either party from presenting their side to the Jurors.
.
----> Paidmediamen mostly succeeds by ensuring that paidmediamen can make audience see what paidmediamen have to say, and other side gets near zero coverage. If other side gets coverage, then paidmediamen will mostly fail to convince their fake stories. But in Jury Trial, each party gets almost equal time in court, and paidmediamen too get some time, to influence Jurors. But there is no way for elitemen to ensure that one side gets zero time. And so paidmediamen campaigns fail to convince falsehoods Jurors.
.
Thats why, paidmediamen are successful in twisting elections in large constituencies. But a candidate cant reach all lakhs and crores voters without paidmediamen. And so paidmediamen decide which one to project. But same paidmediamen fail in local panchayat level elections, because here candidate can reach all voters and so paidmediamen lose their monopoly.
.
Same way, in Jury Trials, paidmediamen cant stop either side from REACHING Jurors. And so paidmediamen fail.
.
===========
.
(C) Costs of paidmedia campaigns limits number of cases they can influence

To run a district level paidmedia campaign, the costs are Rs 10 crore to Rs 50 crore, say average of Rs 20 crore.
.
To run state level paidmedia campaigns, costs are Rs 100 crore to Rs 500 crore depending on size of state and nature of issue. Say average is Rs 200 crore.
.
And consider national level campaign like campaign used for JLN aka Janlokpal Nonsense, The paidmedia costs were of the order of Rs 10000 crore. Lets take average national level campaign cost as Rs 2000 crore
.
You may take any other numbers you wish. But pls dont take small numbers like Rs 1 crore for district level media campaign etc. Such numbers are too unrealistic
.
Now there are 3 crore crore pending cases. Say some 2.80 crore as small cases, some 19 lakh are medium cases, and some 1 lakh are large cases. You may take any numbers.
.
The small cases will mostly end at district level, medium cases will end at state level and large cases may go to state / national level.
.
There are about 600 districts, and so there will be about 40000 small cases per district. If district decides to clear all cases within 5 years, then each will run about 8000 cases per year. And one case will take about 2 weeks. So some 500 courts will be created. And Every month, some 1000 - 1500 cases will be resolved, and in 1 year some 15000 to 18000 cases will be resolved. And in 5 years some 80000 cases will be resolved in each district.
.
And all cases are separate parties. And most cases will be based on different laws and issues.
.
So one campaign CAN NOT effect several cases. This important to note, and so I will repeat. One district / state / national campaign cannot effect too many case. So elitemen decide to influence Jurors using paidmediamen, then they will need to raise paidmedia campaign round the year.
.
So say it tales Rs 20 crore to run a district level paidmedia campaign. There are 280 lakh small cases. So to influence all of them, elitemen will be Rs 5600 lakh crores !!! This is almost 28 times the whole GDP of India. To influence even 10% cases, they will be Rs 560 lakh crore rupees, which is almost 2.8 times the whole GDP. And to influence even 1% cases, elitemen will need Rs 56 lakh crore --- almost 25% India's GDP. (GDP of India is about Rs 200 lakh crores)
.
How much money can elitemen spend after paidmediamen?
.
GDP of India is Rs 200 lakh crore The total advertisement spend across India across print / tv / net was Rs 70000 crore in year 2017-2018. So how much can elitemen spend for paidmedia campaigns?
.
My estimate -- no more than Rs 2 lakh crores a year i.e. at most Rs 10 lakh crores in 5 years. Then how many district level campaigns can they run? Each district level campaign would cost Rs 20 crore. So they can run at most Rs 10,00,000 / 20 = 100,000/2 = 50000 campaigns only. So out of 2.80 crore district level small cases, they can run campaign for only 50000 cases i.e. less than 0.25% cases
.
So in 99.75% cases, elitemen wont be even able to run any paidmedia campaign. And in 0.25% cases, where they did manage to run paidmedia campaigns, in most cases, when other side puts facts, the effect of media campaigns may get washed away.
.
So FINALLY, how many cases can elitemen effect by burning money on paidmediamen? Imo, it is coming below 0.01%
.
Also, elitemen may be able to spend Rs 2 lakh crore a year, as of TODAY. Once MRCM comes, their nefarious incomes from minerals will be less than 5% from what is today. And once MRCM and wealthtax comes, the value of their plots will be 10% of what it is today. A big portion of their nefarious income --- lands and minerals will reduce by 90% to 95%. So they wont be able to spare even Rs 20000 crore for paidmediamen. And with that, they cant run even 1000 district campaigns a year, and so using paidmediam, they cant influence even 0.01% of all 2.80 crore small cases.
.
The NUMERICAL analysis for meidum and large cases will be almost the same.
.
The medium cases will to go to state level.To influence a state level case, the state level paidmedia campaign would cost Rs 200 crore on an average to elitemen. There will be some say some 19 lakh middle level cases. The cost to run paidmedia campaigns for all these cases will be Rs 3800 lakh crores, almost 20 times the whole GDP of India !! With budget of Rs 200,000 crores a year, they conduct campaigns for only 1000 cases i.e. only 5000 cases in five years !!!
.
And again, MRCM and wealthtax will crush their ability to come up with so much money. Most they will be able to spare is Rs 20000 crore a year. And with that, they can barely run 100 state level campaigns in a year and 500 in five years. So in 18,99,500 cases, there will be zero paidmedia campaign. And in 500 cases, where they have have paidmedia campaign to influence Jurors, they cant stop either sides from presenting their arguments. So final success rate will be below 0.01% cases or even lower.
.
And same analysis is for large national level 1 lakh cases
,
===============
.
(D) Tcp can overule Jury verdict
------------------------------------------
.
And in #JurySys3 , a judgment of Jurors can be overturned by Tcp. So elitemen may need to influence half the whole population of district / state / nation, and that too in 1000s of cases. How much money is needed to influence lakhs / crores of voters? Well, even if elitemen get all wealth of India multiplies by 10, they cant run so many campaigns
.
==============
.
(E) How elitemen get high success ratio in judge system?
-------------------------------
.
It is using powers of supreme court judges over 20000 lower court judges !!!
.
So elitemen dont always bribe all 20000 lower court judges, They do, but some 5% to 10% may go otherwise. Then supreme judges are used to bring them in line.
.
So in judge system in India, apex elitemen only need to cover 30 SCjs.
.
But in JurySys, all 3 crore Juries are independent.
.
===============
..
(F) My conclusion:
-------------------
.
cost of district level campaign = Rs 20 crore
cost of state level campaign = Rs 200 crore
cost of national level campaign = Rs 200 crore

number of small district level cases = 2.80 crore
number of medium state level cases = 19 lakhs
number of large national cases = 1 lakh
.
say all cases will be resolved in 5 years
.
India's TOTAL GDP = Rs 200 lakh crores per year
India's total ad spend tv, print etc = Rs 70000 crore per year

Money elitemen can spend after media = ATMOST Rs 200,000 crores per year
.
(Money they will be able to spare after MRCM / wealthtax comes = Rs 20000 crores atmost. But lets take Rs 200,000 crore for now)
.
Money elitemen can spend for paidmedia campaign in 5 years = Rs 10,00,000 crores at most
.
number of small cases they run campaign for = Rs 10,00,000 crore / Rs 20 crore = 50000 atmost = 0.2% = 2 out of 1000 !! So in 998 out of 1000 small cases, they wont be able to even run a campaign.
.
number of middle level small cases they run campaign for = Rs 10,00,000 crore / Rs 200 crore = 5000 atmost = 0.3% = 3 out of thousand !! So in 997 out of 1000 small cases, they wont be able to even run a campaign.
.
number of large level small cases they run campaign for = Rs 10,00,000 crore / Rs 2000 crore = 500 atmost = 0.5% = 5 out of thousand !! So in 995 out of 1000 small cases, they wont be able to even run a campaign.
.
And in 5 out of 1000 cases, where they might be able to have campaign ---- what are the chances that campaign can actually turn Jurors' opinions? Subjective, but neglible,.
.
So objective part is --- in 995 out of 1000 Jury Trials, elitemenj wont be even able to run a paidmedia campaign !! And chances that their paidmedia campaign can effect Jurors in 5 out of 1000 cases are also small.
.
IOW, effect of elitemen's paidmedia camapains on 3 crore Jury Trials across India will be = less than 5 out 1000, or may be less than 1 out of 10000 !!!
.
Now compare that with 99.99% success elitemen have in judge system !!!
.
So my point is ---- those who claim that #JurySys3 will worsen India compared to EXISTING judge system --- I dismiss that as a LIE.....

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 03 2018 13:48:33 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

common FALLACIES used by anti-recallists and pseudo-recallists to defame RTR / Jury / MRCM etc law-drafts, and how a NUMERICAL analysis can rebut their fake claims
.
Pls see list of fallaices at https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_fallacies
.
And in particular, pls see fallacy of composition --- https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fallacy_of_composition .
.
see wiki ---- " The fallacy of composition arises when one infers that something is true of the whole from the fact that it is true of some part of the whole (or even of every proper part). "
.
Fallacies of compsition in extereme is --- a person will claim a statement that may be true in just 1 out 10000 cases ; and then he will apply them on ALL 10000 cases !!!
.
A similar is fallacy of hasty generalization --- https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Faulty_generalization#Hasty_generalization
.
Hasty generalization usually shows the pattern

X is true for A.
X is true for B.
Therefore, X is true for C, D, E, etc.
.
===========
.
So here is how plot of pseudo-recallists or anti-recallists goes .
.
(1) elitemen will use paidmediamen to influence Jurors --- fine. But in how many of the 3 crore cases, can they create a campaign? Because each case has different parties, different issue and different context. So one campaign cant influence even 5 cases, and all 5 cases may not be running on same day in same district !!!
.
(2) How much money district level paidmedia campaigns, state level paidmedia campaigns and national level paidmedia campaigns cost?
.
(3) how much money can elitemen spare today? And how much can they spare after MRCM and wealthtax weakens them?
.
(4) so what % of 3 crore cases can they influence?
.
It turns out --- in 99.5% cases, they cant even finance a paidmedia campaign to influence Jurors !!
.
And in 5 out of 1000 cases, where they may be able to manage the campaign, in how many cadses will paidmedia campaign effect Jurors? Are they assuming without stating that all Jurors are dumb and will ignore arguments given by parties in court, and will listen only to paidmedia?
.
When all these factors are put , it will show that paidmedia can change judgment of Jurors in less than 1 in 10000 cases and will have NO effect in 9999 of the 10000 cases
.
And the possible effect in 1 out of 10000 cases is mentioned, and then applies on ALL 10000 cases
.
rebuttal is to do a NUMERICAL ANALYSIS of how much money elitemen can spare on paidmediamen, costs of paidmedia campaigns at district / state / national levels and how much money elitemen have today, and will have after MRCM , wealthtax and RTR/JurySys comes. And apply SUBJECTIVE part --- in how many cases can paidmedia wash effect of statements made by both parties.
.
When all numbers are put on paper, it becomes clear that they may be true in at most 1 out of 10000 cases, or even less. ...
.
---
.
And I also have a "bad" news --- other than NUMERICAL analysis, and that too sometimes MULTI-DIMENSIONAL numerical analysis, there is no way to show that false objections created by using fallacies of generalizations and fallacies of compositions , are false. So dear recallists, pls always keep a calculator with you !!! There are no short cuts - be life or business or activism.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 03 2018 14:33:40 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How RTR-law-draft such as #RtrDeo3 forces all DEOs to improve their own DEO websites
.
There are about 600 to 700 DEO = District Education Officers in India. I have proposed lawdraft https://newindia . in/causes/RtrDep3 which will create a procedure by which voters of district can replace DEO. Pls download draft from the above link.
.
The procedure enables a DEO to become DEO of upto 5 districts across state and 10 across India, if they have approvals of voters. And if they are DEOs of N districts, then their salaries will N times. And also, their chances of getting appointed State Education Minister by voters increases later
.
So now , how will DEO's websites will improve once RTR-DEO comes?
.
Say one DEO A improves website. Then ALL voters across India will be able to see, and will ask their DEOs to improve their respective website. And if he doesnt, then chances that citizens will approve A and replace their DEO with A increases
.
Now website will NOT be only factor. There are many factors, like maths / science exams, teaching material provides, how payments to teachers / students etc is scheduled and so on.

And the draft has provision of comparing DNA of suspected Bangldeshies in India with their possible blood relatives in Bangladesh. And draft also has provision of taking NARCOTEST in public of Bangldeshi suspect. The draft is in PDF attachment. If you support this draft, then pls vote on it, and share the link, and take other steps

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Sep 04 2018 07:37:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

#Minerals3 , Minerals1 aka – MRCM (Mineral Royalty for Citizens and Military) law-draft at https://newindia.in/causes/Minerals3 , https://newindia.in/causes/Minerals1
.
सेना और नागरिकों के लिए खनिज रोयालिटी
( हिंदी अनुवाद अंग्रेजी ड्राफ्ट बाद दिया है )
.
--- start of law-draft ----
.
(1) Within 4 months these law-draft is printed in Gazette by PM, 65% of the money, coming to Central Govt of India as [ (1a) royalty for minerals + (1b) royalty of spectrum + (1c) land rent from land owned by Central Govt ], but not money from waters, divided by number of adult citizens of India, will be directly deposited into adult citizens’ bank accounts.
.
(2) The remaining 35% will be spent to improve Military and other Govt services.
.
---- end of law-draft -----
.
summary of the law (pls note - this is summary, and not part of the draft, and not binding on anyone, and it isnt a promise either. )
.
(a) Say this proposed law is printed in Gazette by PM say in jan-2019. Say mineral royalty , spectrum royalty and land rent from government owned plots in the month of say feb-2019 is say Rs 210000 crore. Then as per this law-draft, after it is printed in Gazette, about Rs 70000 crore in march-2019 will be given for the budget of Military. And about Rs 140000 crore will be divided almost equally amongst 90 crore adult citizens of India i.e. each citizen will get around Rs 1550 rupee in march-2019.
.
(b) But if mineral royalties and land rent in next month were say Rs 150,000 crores, then each adult citizen may get about Rs 1100.
.
(c) So this law does NOT gurantee any fixed monthly sum to each citizen. Every month, all citizens will get DIRECT DEPOSITS from money that govt gets from mineral royalties, spectrum royalties and land rent from all the govt plots.
.
(d) This law-draft may reduce poverty to near zero level within just 3 to 6 months.
.
--- start of law-draft ----
.
(1) इस कानून को प्रधानमंत्री द्वारा राजपत्र में छापने के 4 महीनो के अन्दर भारत सरकार के पास ( खनिज आमदनी + स्पेक्ट्रम आमदनी + भारत सरकारकी जमीनों का किराया ) के रूप में प्राप्त होने वाली 65% राशि को भारत के समस्त वयस्क नागरिक की संख्या से विभाजित करने पर हिस्से में आया पैसा सीधे वयस्क नागरिक के बैंक खाते में जमा होगा।
.
(2) शेष 35% हिस्से का उपयोग सेना तथा अन्य सरकारी सेवाए में सुधार के लिए खर्च होगा।
.
------- end of law-draft ------
.
प्रस्तावित क़ानून का सार ( कृपया ध्यान दें कि -- यह सिर्फ इस ड्राफ्ट का सार है , एवं यह क़ानून ड्राफ्ट का हिस्सा नहीं है। यह किसी भी व्यक्ति पर बाध्यकारी नहीं है और न ही यह किसी प्रकार का कोई वचन है।
.
(a) मान लीजिये कि भारत के प्रधानमंत्री द्वारा यह क़ानून जनवरी-2019 में गेजेट में छाप दिया जाता है। मान लीजिये कि खनिज रोयल्टी, स्पेक्ट्रम रोयल्टी और सरकारी जमीन का किराया फरवरी-2019 के लिए 210,000 करोड़ रूपये है। तो इस क़ानून ड्राफ्ट के अनुसार मार्च-2019 में 70000 करोड़ रूपये सेना को तथा शेष 140000 करोड़ रुपये भारत के 90 करोड़ नागरिको के खाते में सीधे जमा किये जायेंगे। इस तरह मार्च-2019 में प्रत्येक व्यस्क भारतीय के खाते में 1550 रूपये जमा होंगे।
.
(b) लेकिन मानिये अगले महीने खनिज रोयल्टी, स्पेक्ट्रम रोयल्टी और सरकारी जमीन का किराया फरवरी-2019 के लिए 150,000 करोड़ रूपये है। तो प्रत्येक व्यस्क भारतीय के खाते में 1100 रूपये जमा होंगे।
.
(c) दूसरे शब्दों में खनन रोयल्टी, स्पेक्ट्रम रोयल्टी एवं सरकारी भूमि का किराया प्रत्येक महीने भारत के प्रत्येक व्यस्क नागरिक के खाते में सीधे जमा होगा। लेकिन कोई निश्चित रकमकी गेरेंटी नहीं है.
.
(d) इस व्यवस्था के आने से सिर्फ 3-6 महीने के भीतर भारत में भुखमरी और गरीबी की समस्या कमहो सकती है. ]
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Tue Sep 04 2018 17:54:51 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

सभी रिकालिस्ट्स के लिए महत्त्वपूर्ण सूचना ;
.
राईट टू रिकॉल के नाम पर कुछ छद्म कार्यकर्ता नागरिको को फोन करके उनसे अपनी निजी वेबसाइट पर sms करने या मिस कॉल करने को कहते है, और इनसे इनका वोटर नंबर भी मांगते है। राजनैतिक रूप से सक्रीय लोगो का डेटा इकट्ठा करके ये छद्म कार्यकर्ता ये जानकारी कांग्रेस / बीजेपी / आम आदमी पार्टी को बेच देते है !!!
.
हमें कुछ नागरिको ने रिपोर्ट किया है कि उन्हें अचानक अपने फोन पर आम आदमी पार्टी के मेसेज आने लगे एवं उनके घर पर आम आदमी पार्टी के पर्चे भी पोस्ट किये गए। मालूम करने पर पता चला कि जिस वेबसाइट पर उन्होंने राइट टू रिकॉल, जूरी सिस्टम कानूनों को सपोर्ट करने के लिए sms भेजा था एवं अपना वोटर नंबर दिया था उन्होंने कुछ हजार रूपये में यह डेटा आम आदमी पार्टी की स्थानीय इकाई को बेच दिया था। चुनाव करीब आने के साथ ही अब उन्होंने डेटा इकट्ठी करने की अपनी गतिविधियां तेज कर दी है।
.
ये छद्म कार्यकर्ता ज्यादातर निम्न तरीके एवं चरणों का इस्तेमाल करते है --

१) ज्योंही वे आपको फेसबुक पर राइट टू रिकॉल कानूनों का समर्थन करते देखेंगे , वे आपको इनबॉक्स में आकर मोबाइल नंबर मांगेंगे और आपसे फोन पर बात करने की कोशिश करेंगे।
.
२) वे आपको कानूनों का लिंक देंगे उनमे अपनी प्राइवेट वेबसाइट का लिंक होगा और वे जो आपको फेसबुक पोस्ट भेजेंगे उनमे भी उनकी प्राइवेट वेबसाइट का लिंक होगा। इस तरह वो जानकारी देने के नाम पर आपको अपनी वेबसाइट पर धकेलने की कोशिश करेंगे।
.
३) अंत में उनका लक्ष्य यह रहेगा कि आप उनकी वेबसाइट पर पाने वास्तविक नंबर से एक sms भेजे।
.
४) अंतिम चरण में वे आपसे आपका वोटर नंबर मांगेंगे और उसे अपनी वेबसाइट पर रखेंगे।
.
इस तरह वे आपको कानूनों की जानकारी देने के नाम पर आपको अपनी प्राइवेट वेबसाइट पर भेजेंगे कर आपसे आपका वास्तविक फोन नंबर , वोटर नंबर निकलवा लेंगे। जब उनको आपके वोटर नंबर मिल जाएंगे तो वे चुनाव आयोग की वेबसाइट पर जाकर इससे आपका पता निकाल लेते है।
.
ये राजनैतिक रूप से बेहद छांटा हुआ डेटा है। क्योंकि यह "राजनैतिक रूप से सक्रीय" उन लोगो का डेटा है जो अभी किसी भी पार्टी से संबद्ध नहीं है , और विकल्प की तलाश में है। इस वजह से राजनैतिक पार्टियां इस डेटा को हाथो हाथ खरीद लेती है। साथ ही इनके वेबसाइट पर राजनैतिक विषय पर sms , miss call , एवं सर्च ट्रेफिक , विजिटर बढ़ने से इनकी वेबसाइट का मूल्य बढ़ता जाता है। जब इनकी वेबसाइट पर ट्रेफिक बेहद बढ़ जाएगा तो ये लोग अपनी पूरी वेबसाइट किसी राजनैतिक पार्टी को बेचकर रवाना हो जाएंगे। गौर तलब बात यह है कि इन वेबसाइटों पर मालिक का नाम नहीं लिखा हुआ होता है। वे अपनी पहचान छुपा कर रखते है और दूसरा व्यक्ति बनकर आप से बात करते है।
.
जल्दी ही मैं राईट टू रिकॉल के नाम पर इस तरह का डेटा इकट्ठा करके बेचने के धंधे में लगे हुए छद्म कार्यकर्ताओ की सूची एवं उनकी वेबसाइट्स के लिंक सार्वजनिक करूँगा।
.
कार्यकताओ से मेरा आग्रह है कि -- यदि कोई कार्यकर्ता आपसे इनबॉक्स में या सार्वजनिक रूप से आपके नंबर मांगता है तो उससे चौकन्ने रहे। एवं यदि वह राइट टू रिकॉल कानूनों का समर्थन बनने के ढोंग करता है आपसे किसी भी प्राइवेट वेबसाइट पर sms करने को कहता है , तो right to recall india के एडमिन्स को रिपोर्ट करें।
.
राइट टू रिकॉल इंडिया ग्रुप का लिंक --- <https://www.facebook.com/groups/rtrindia/>;
.
उल्लेखनीय है कि राइट टू रिकॉल ग्रुप एक विकेन्द्रित व्यवस्था के तहत काम करता है एवं हमारे कार्यकर्ता कभी भी नागरिको को किसी निजी वेबसाइट पर sms भेजने को नहीं कहते है। हम अपने सभी ड्राफ्ट सिर्फ फेसबुक एवं सरकारी वेबसाइट newindia पर रखते है। कुछ दो साल पहले जब राइट टू रिकॉल के एक छद्म कार्यकर्ता ने राइट टू रिकॉल के विभिन्न कार्यकर्ताओ के माध्यम से नागरिको का डेटा इकट्ठा करवाकर बेच दिया था , तब से हमने किसी भी प्राइवेट वेबसाइट का इस्तमाल करना छोड़ दिया। लेकिन जो छद्म रिकालिस्ट इस कारोबार में है , वे आज भी यह धंधा कर रहे है।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Sep 05 2018 06:58:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Managing SPURIOUS proposed-variations in proposed-law-drafts
.
One thing we recallists worked is --- we gave DRAFTS and so those who were giving blue sky arguments were on backfoot. .
But draft based movement can be sabotaged by throwing VARIATIONS after variations on all proposed-law-drafts. And person who is proposing variation can keep throwing claims Original Draft is bad and only his draft with variation is good. And then next person can claim that Original draft is bad, draft plus first variation is also bad, and only draft with first variation and his proposed second variation is good. That way, recallists, will remain confined to law-draft-analysis and will never be able to execute campaign steps. So anti-recallists win !!
.
After all, activism is law-drafts PLUS campaign steps. If all recallists decide to confine to law-draft-analysis and dont spare time and energy for campaign-steps, then law-drafts will never get printed.
.
----------
.
So how can recallists deal with proposed-variations on proposed-law-drafts and decide whether to accept / reject variation WITHOUT centralization?
.
Solution I propose is following
.
(1) Consider proposed-original-draft-A and proposed-campaign-steps-B. Say recallists are executing campaign-steps-B.
.
(2) Say person-X is proposing (draft-A + variation-V). Variation can be addition, deletion or modification of clauses. Or, it can be mere re-ordering of clauses.
.
(3a) If person-X says "draft-A is good and draft-A + variation is better" , then there is no BLOCKING. The recallists who agree can continue campaigns steps for (A + V). And rest may ignore V
.
(3b) But say person-X claims that "draft-A is bad and A+V is good", then essentially, he asking all recallists to STOP taking campaign-steps-B. THIS CAN BE DUE TO INTENTION OF SABOTAGE. OR CAN BE BONAFIDE.
.
[e.g.1 When Janlokpal Nonsense Draft was proposed, I had said that "Janlokpal Draft " is bad and only "Janlokpal with RTR Janlokpal clauses" is good. So was asking all to stop campaigning for Janlokpal only and campaign ONLY for "Janlokpal plus RTR Janlokpal". Now we know that Janplokpal was a nonsense, and just a drama.
.
e.g.2 When aadhar system was proposed in year 2011, I mentioned that system has NOTHING to stop even NEW-COMING Bangladeshies !! So I had proposed aadhar plus amendment of linking one's aadhar with relatives' aadhar . But I did NOT oppose standalone Aadhar, because Aadhar alone to reduces fund siphoning, criminal tracking, finding lost children etc. So my stand was "support standalone aadhar" and "support aadhar plus amendments".]
.
So if situation is (3a), then there is no issue to be addressed. But if situation is (3b), then

------------
.
SOLUTION?
.
Then following questions a recallists can ask to decide whether he should accept person-X's demand to stop campaign for draft-A or continue.
.
(Q1). Ask person-X to give details of campaign steps he proposes. i.e. what steps does he ask say 80 crore voters to take in case say these 80 crore voters decide to support (A + V). If campaign steps are absent, then chances are high that his sole goal is only to stop draft-A , not promote force PM to print (draft-A + V)
.
(Q2) Apply quantitative analysis to decide whether draft-A alone will IMPROVE from EXISTING situation we have TODAY or worsen from existing situation. If draft-A alone can improve situation which we have TODAY, then his motives behind opposing ORIGINAL DRAFT A needs to be speculated
.
(Q3) person-X will give some scenarios in which draft-A worsens existing situation and (A + V) improves. See if his claim of WORSENING is valid / invalid in what % of possible cases.
.

So QUANTITATIVE analysis of his campaign methods and (A + V) will enable a recallist to decide without centralization, whether person-X's proposal to stop campagn for law-draft-A is malafine or bonafide.

Opposing RTR-law-drafts by putting pre-conditions (or co-conditions)
.
Ever since I proposed RTR-law-drafts in oct-1998 , I have faced these litanies by those who oppose RTR that "RTR is bad without my law-drafts / pre-conditions and RTR is good but ONLY with my law-drafts / preconditions " !!
.
I dismiss their statements as nonsense. They are as bad as people who say "RTR is bad. PERIOD" !!!
.
Lets say just one RTR-law-draft that I have proposed is printed. Then almost all RTR-law-drafts have TCP-clauses aka CV-clauses. And those CV-clauses aka TCP-clauses are as powerful as standalone TCP. The power of TCP-clauses dont reduce or increase whether they are embedded in a larger draft or standalone. And TCP-clauses alone can ensure that MRCM-law-draft gets printed within a few months and so on.
.
And lets take any RTR-draft munus TCP-clauses. That draft alone too will force every nodal officer (on whom RTR-draft has been printed) in district / state / India to improve his department and improve his staff
.
All these claims that "RTR-drafts without my preconditions" are nonsense. And there are too many people putting 10s of conditions. I wont find time to address even one of them
.
But all of you may notice --- for the new draft they give (copy of original + conditions) , they refuse to mention what steps 90 CRORE voters of India should take to COMMUNICATE to PmoIndia to print their proposed law !! If anyone is serious about getting any law-draft printed, the FIRST thing he will ask law-draft-supporter to COMMUNICATE TO PmoIndia to print those law-draft. Absence of this step shows that they have no intention of getting their own pet law-draft printed !! IOW, they only want badmounth existing RTR-law-drafts by giving weird scenarios in which RTR-law-draft will fail, and their magic preconditions and magic additions will work as savior !!!
.
So there are 40 lakh RSS-workers who oppose RTR-drafts, some 10 lakh congress-workers who oppose RTR and 2 lakh AAP-workers who oppose RTR. A few dozen more --- makes no difference.
.
Now why I wont rebut all 1000 "RTR-law-draft is bad arguments"? Because I have 10s of work to do. In RTR, I have to upload 10 main drafts, upload 50 other drafts, REGISTER PARTY, work to train candidates in form filling etc, write 40 pamphlets, on 10 main law-drafts etc. And I have to earn money to give newspaper advertisements, and also pay for my own expenses. And I have plenty of other personal work.
.
Overtime, I will write on how recallists can address the FAKE scenarios that RTR-draft-opposers give. In general, one should take QUANTITATIVE approach in all the steps in their scenario. One the opposer is asked to give QUANTITIVE DETAILS, the nonsense of his scenario that claims that RTR-law-drafts are bad will become clear. And this way, his scenario can be falsified to the audience. ..

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Sep 05 2018 07:00:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Statistical Verifiability vs Specific Verifiability vs Universal Specific Verifiability vs General Feeling
.
For activism, Statistical Verifiability and General Feeling suffice. Only Law / Punishment / Courts etc need Specific Verifiability
.
(the topic is imp because many activists stall campaign steos by insisting on Specific Verifiability in activism, which is NOT needed at all)

Say I ask you question "what % of traffic constables take bribe - (a) below 20%, (b) between 20% to 40% .... (f) over 80% ". Then most will say "over 80%" or "between 60% and 80%". Now none ever tried to "verify" thus statistically or specifically
,.
Statistical Verification will be --- make list of all 1 lakh traffic constables in India, select say 1000 at random using PRIME NUMBERS and then verify them.
.
And SPECIFIC VERIFICATION will be verifying each and every of the 1 lakh.
.
--------------
.
Next question can be "how many voters in India believe that leader-X is better than leader-Y for the position of PM"?
.
Again, one can make a list of ALL 90 crore voters and tally their opinion.
.
But if an activist is looking for answer, then he doesnt need to get opinions of all 90 crore voters,. He can get list of 90 crore voters, and select say 10000 at random and ask them by contacting them. Or, a group of activists can first select ONE list of 10000 voters , and then each can visit the specific voters selected in his area
.
The key is -- whether selection was truely random or not? And did it assume ensure that each voter is EQUALLY likely to be selected after N voters were selected?
.
And one can keep selection procedure OPEN to all. That can be done by using public domain codes, which generates large random numbers and large prime numbers
.
Law / courts etc do need SPECIFIC VERIFIABILITY. eg lets say that statistical verification show that 80% IAS have assets more than income. Then we can NOT then select 80% of all at random and imprison them !! Here, we need SPECIFIC verification.
.
Likewise, if we want to decide Loksabha Member based on popularity, then again we can NOT use opinion polls, stakes here are so high, that govt officers may get bribed to pick non random and biased sample. So here we need UNIVERSAL POLL.
.
But say activist on his own wants to decide which candidate is more popular. Then he doesnt need to visit all 15 lakh voters in that constituency. He can decide by choosing 1000-2000 voters at random from the list. The activist wants to know for himself and so he has no reason to take a biased sample. .
.
Now what if two activist differ? Non-issue. Activists want to CHANGE opinion of voters and so for them it doesnt matter what is existing opinion of voters.
.
--------------
.
If Mahatma Udham Singh wants to know "what % of voters support law-draft-A", then he can take random sample and verify himself. Or when, % of supporters is high, GENERAL FEELING will suffice. eg Udham Singh didnt do any door to door survey asking 25 crore adult Indian citizens , asking them "shall I execute Governor Dwyer"? He did NOT need to take such survey. The GENERAL FEELING was sufficient that a huge majority of Indian citizens back them wanted execution of Governor Dwyer. So he went by GENERAL FEELING
.
Sometimes general feeling can be fake, and mere creation of paidmediamen. But activist can take a small sample from ENTIRE VOTER LIST of INDIA.
.
------------
.
We at Right to Recall Party work to inform voters about law-drafts, and then if they decide to bring these law-drafts, then we request voters to DIRECTLY ask PM / CM / MP / MLA to print these laws. And then come 10s of other steps. We need to spend time in UNDERSTANDING drafts, explaining drafts and also mention how voters can DIRECTLY communicate to PM and other voters. "what % of voters have PM to print law-draft-X" becomes important when % is above to 50%.IOW, verifiability of CLAIM is needed ONLY after % is high. And some people want activists to spend a lot of time TODAY for verifaibility,.
.
So my request is --- if your goal is to get law-draft-X printed, then pls work on verifiablility after YOU are convinced that % of those who asked PM to prmt law-draft X has exceded 50%.. Till then, verification or no verification has no value to Udham Singh or anyone. ...

Making all RELEVANT govt information available to all --- why this needs RTR / JurySys first
.
Can we have setup where each voter gets access to relevant information within govt , without RTR / Jury?
.
My answer is --- NO.
.
Because unless there is threat on officers that citizens can PUNISH them if they were later caught hiding relevant information, the officers will hide as much as they can.
.
And most disclosure rules can evaded
.
eg when audit of Govt book improved, the Ministers / officers / judges promptly started giving more and more contract work to private companies, whose accounts are prima-facie onfidential !! And then they started en-masse privatization
.
eg some 10 years back, it was possible for Railway Ministers and Officers to siphone funds allocated for station maintenance and hide it. Today, since all books are on computers, it is difficult to hide. So what did Ministers do? They calmly started PRIVATIZING railway station. So now books are maintained by private companies, which will further
give contract to private comp[anies,. and this most details never come on govt records.
.
And consider the cases of MISUSE of discretionary powers. The officer can always cook data that will look use of
discretionary power bonafide, until a Jury looks into it.
.
To give one example --- in many areas, 1000 workers are hired, and 500 work and 500 sit. The sitters take money and just give 75% to 90% back to Govt officers !! Now all 1000 entries are valid real humans with aadhar and with finger prints, and all money goes via bank. Unless Jury or voters actually do a TRIAL, they will never come to informed
conclusion that 1000 people were hired to do a task where only 500 were needed.
.
So first, unless there is RTR / Jury on officers, they will put information in English only as far as possible, and in clumsiest possible way that meets the written criteria, making information practically inaccessible, overloaded and useless. As a software developer, I can say that one can never codify "user friendliness" and it comes ONLY from market competition where users expel a bad product and promote a good product, So only way to make officers put information in USER FRIENDLY way is --- citizens need to expel the officers who dont put all relevant information in user friendly way, and citizens need to promote those who do display maximal relevant information in maximal user friendly way. IOW, imo,
without RTR / Jury, the dream that officers will display all RELEVANT information in user friendly way is just a pipe dream. Thats why, I have made RTR primary. ...

Killing draft based movement by throwing series of spurious ADDITIONS (and variations) to proposed-drafts
.
Ours is a proposed-law-draft-based movement. We have proposed some 200-250 law-drafts. Almost each law-draft has Tcp clauses using which they can extended once passed. In fact, once any law-draft is passed, the Tcp clauses can be used to bring ALL other 200 law-drafts !! eg say #RrtrDeo3 is printed. Then using Tcp-clauses (aka CV-clauses) , activists can work to bring unrelated proposed-law-drafts like DDMCAM as well.
.
The one and ONLY question --- will printing a DRAFT as is, without any addition or variation, without any pre-condition or co-condition ----- will it DAMAGE India or improve India?
.
The same question can be put as ---- will situation of India improve after draft is printed or worsen? Will India / China and India / USA strength ratio improve of worsen?
.
So I will make my stand clear ---- imo, these drafts will improve condition of India i.e.. no draft will worsen condition of India along any dimension - education, manufacturing, weapon manufacturing etc.
.
And improvement is never ending process. More technology will bring more methods to criminals and enemies, and more laws will needed to deal with them.
.
------------------
.
But lately I have come across an interesting way to KILL our draft based movement ---- keep asking for additions of clauses till each 2 page to 10 page draft becomes a 50 page drafts.
.
Their FUD (Fear Uncertainty Doubt) technique is --- each ORIGINAL DRAFT is shown as something that will ruin India if passed, and then (draft + addition) is shown as magic remedy !!!
.
This is not new to me. When I first proposed RTR law-drafts in 1999, the typical congress/rss/aap (aap = cpi back then) claim was " unless India has 90% education of 70% education, these drafts will ruin India" !! And then "unless people of India FIRST become as nationalistic as people of Japan / USA, these RTR-drafts will ruin India". IOW, some pre-condition will be thrown as magic remedy and sentence will have praise of RTR-drafts, but praise will be followed by a "but ..." and then a pre-condition meant to waste time
.
-------------
.
HOW TO DEAL WITH SUCH ADDITION and CONDITION THROWER
.
(1) In each case, first see whether ORIGINAL DRAFT (minus additions proposed) will improve India or worsen India? It goes without saying that there are 100 possible additions and each addition will cause some improvement, Thats why almost all drafts have Tcp or CV clauses so that activists can add clauses or new laws with lesser eforts
.
(2) if you think that the ORIGINAL DRAFT (minus proposed additions) will improve India, then support the draft. And dismiss the persons (who claims that "Original Draft will ruin India , unless my proposed additions are added") as a LIAR and move on
.
(3) If you oppose ORIGINAL DRAFT, then fine. You can decide your own course ...

How anti-recallists or psuedo-recallists use exaggerated strength of paidmediamen create fear against proposed RTR-law-drafts - by creating a FALSE scenario that elitemen can create thousands and lakhs of paidmedia campaigns !!!
.
One of the arguments of anti-recallists (or psuedo-recallists) use against all proposed RTR-law-drafts is --- (a) RTR-law-drafts and JurySys-law-drafts put power in hands of voters, and (b) paidmediamen can manipulate voter opinions and Juror opinions and so RTR/Jury-law-drafts MUST be postponed till XYZ conditions are met !!
.
Their main plank is --- they show paidmediamen as supremely powerful, more powerful than 10 Gods, who can convince voters that day is night and night is day !!!
.
And THEY IGNORE THE COST THAT PAIDMEDIA CAMPAIGNS NEED.
.
When RTR-law-drafts comes, voters get power to expel / replace / retain / bringback 600 District Education Officers, 600 District Police Commissioners, 600 District judges, 600 District Public Prosecutors i.e. some 25 nodal officers in each district i.e. about 25 * 600 = 150,000 District level offices. And Juries get power to expel all 10000s of Municipal / State officers below the rank of District Nodal Officers such as collectors.
.
And voters get power to replace 30 CMs, 30 High Court Chief judges, 30 State Chief Public Prosecutors, all High Court judges and so on. So there are 30 states, and some 50 to 200 officers / judges in each state (depending on size of State's apex bureaucracy / judiciary ). So at State level , voters get procedure to expel / replace / retain / bringback 30 * 100 = 3000 state level apex officials
.
And at national level they almost all Ministers, apex PSU chairman, SCCj, SCjs, RBI Chairman, RBI Directors, SBI Chairman etc become recallable/replaceable. So at national level, some 20 to 50 positions become recallable / replaceable.
.
Now can paidmediamen manipulate opinion on ALL positions?
.
And if they fail to manipulate opinions even on 10% positions, then the 100% good individuals at apex, using their official powers, can dig out all data and expose remaining 90%. One good judge is enough to expose all nepotism in all courts,. One good Minister is enough to expose murky deals of ALL Ministers.
.
One clean District Education cant get data of other DEOs, but he can surely frame RIGHT QUESTIONS and send to all voters across states, that will expose all DEOs. Worse, a good DEO can also frame right questions that expose ALL police chiefs, all judges, all public prosecutors etc and send these questions to all voters across District / State.
.
So even if paidmedia is successful in putting elitemen's puppets in 90% places, the remaining 10% will do so much expose in 2-3 months, that subsequent recalls will ensure replacement of all their puppets.
.
So once RTR/Jury-law-drafts are printed, elitemen will need to run LAKHS of paidmedia campaign to stop good guys from coming from 100% of 1000s of district level / state level positions.
.
Each paidmedia campaign has to run for several days. And for all those days, 24 hour coverage has to be given. eg consider paidmediamen campaign that was run for Anna during 4-apr-2011 and 11-apr-2011. The campaign had to be run almost 24 hours for seven days. And then weeks and months or coverage had to be given to install ArKe as a leader. The costs of each campaign is 1000s of crores of rupees
.
So when RTR comes over 1000s of district level positions across India, and 10s of State level positions across India, and 10s of National Level positions, the elitemen will need to run 1000s of campaigns every MONTH to ensure that ONLY their puppets come on ALL positions.
.
======================
.
Cost estimates of 1000s of paidmedia campaigns
------------------------------------------------------
.
So lets do how much will it cost elitemen to stop good guys from coning at all positions across India
.
district level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 50 crore at least. state level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 1000 cr average
national level paidmedia campaign cost = Rs 20000 crores. .
.
district level positions = at least 30
state level positions = at least 30
national level positions = at least 20
.
number of districts = 600
number of states = 20 (club many small states as one)
nation = 1
.
so cost of all district level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 30 positions per district * 600 district * Rs 50 crore per campaign
= 18000 * Rs 50 crores
= Rs 900,000 crores
.
so cost of all state level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 30 positions per state * 20 states * Rs 1000 crore per campaign
= 600 * Rs 1000 crores
= Rs 600,000 crores
.
cost of all national level paidmedia campaign to get bad guy in and stop good guy
= 20 positions across India * 1 nation * Rs 20000 crore per campaign
= Rs 400,000 crores
.
Total = Rs 900,000 + Rs 600,000 + Rs 400,000 = Rs 29,00,000 crores !!
.
This is close to ALL taxes collected by center / state / municipalities across India. And this is almost 15% of India's total GDP !!
.
Well, elitemen cant spend even 1/10th of the money they need to block all good guys and putting all their puppets at even 10% of the district / state / national positions.
.
And say elitemen and paidmediamen managed to put their puppets on these positions. But in RTR-drafts, the voters can replace / expel anyday !!! So elitemen will need to spend crores of rupees PER POSITION every week to hide facts about good guys and promote lies about their bad guys. Now you multiply amounts, by number of positions per state / district and number of states / district . No one has such money
.
Another way to analyse is to take per citizen PER POSITION cost. Each citizen has now power to replace / expel / retain bringback people at 80 positions across distroict / state / India. If paidmedia expense per position per voter is Rs 100, then cost to manipulate opnions of 50 crore voters for 80 positions is
= 80 positions * 50 crore voters * Rs 100 per voter per position
= Rs 400,000 crores AT LEAST.
.
And recall / replacement is possible anyday. So everyweek, paidmediamen will need to print lies about puppets and lies about good guys. So the cost now becomes alnost Rs 400,000 PER WEEK !!! . No one has that kind of money/
.
===================
.
So how do elitemen prevail using paidmediamen today?
----------------------------------------------------------------------
.
A voters wants a good PM / CM. And so he has no choice but to vote for MP / MLA candidate of the party to which hos preferred PM candidate belongs to.
.
So defacto, whole nation is electing only one PM, and only 25-30 CMs only !! The election of 543 MPs and 5000 MLAs is only a formality to elect that one MP and 25 - 30 CMs !!! And election of PM / CM comes only once in 5 years,
.
So number of POSITIONS for which voters EFFECTIVELY vote today are few --- only 2 per state (PM / CM). So today, elitemen and paidmediamen prevail due to lack of voting opportunities that voters have.
.
And at PM / CM level, voters dont have "word of mouth" to know performance,. eg Voters of Ahmedabad can know about performance of DEO of Rajkot thru his friends etc in Rajkot. So PAIDMEDIA campaign will have zero effect in DEO campaigns - because voters have firsthand info from people they know. But in PM / CM, population is so large, and number of tasks are so many --- that one cant evaluate. So one goes by 2-3 major problems like crimes, law-order, islamist problem etc, and casts his votes. And so paidmediamen prevail.
.
=============
.
IOW, all the claims that paidmediamen can manipulate opinions of 90 voters for even 10% of the positions of DEO, district police chiefs, district judges, district public prosecutors, state HCCj, HCjs, CMs, PM, RBI Governor, SBI Chairman, ONGC Chairman is just a WHITE LIE. Elietemen dont have money to manipulate so many opnions,.
.
And claim that elitemen can manipulate opinions of crores Jurors in 3 crore Jury Trial , thru paidmedia, is a bigger LIE. And LIE becomes clear when costs are calculated per trial. I will explain in some other posts. ...
